- **public-02-claude-skills-main-marketing-linkedin-skills-linkedin-analytics-references-evidence-thresholds-a24b35b9**: The characteristic sentence of LinkedIn analytics is: "carousels do 3x better for me." It is usually built on four posts. With engagement as heavy-tailed as it is, four posts will show a 3x difference between almost any two groups you care to define — including groups defined by the first letter of the first word. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-marketing-linkedin-skills-linkedin-analytics-references-evidence-thresholds-a24b35b9))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-marketing-linkedin-skills-linkedin-analytics-references-linkedin-metrics-canon-f1cd55b3**: LinkedIn gives creators a small set of numbers and almost no definitions. The result is a advice ecosystem built on metrics whose meaning has changed at least twice, compared across periods where they meant different things. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-marketing-linkedin-skills-linkedin-analytics-references-linkedin-metrics-canon-f1cd55b3))

- **public-02-claude-skills-main-marketing-linkedin-skills-linkedin-content-references-accessibility-and-inclusion-4c20e9bc**: LinkedIn is a professional network, which means a meaningful fraction of your audience uses assistive technology at work — screen readers, captions, magnification, high-contrast modes. It also means the accessibility failures in LinkedIn posts are unusually visible, because they are almost all self-inflicted by formatting tricks people adopt for reach. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-marketing-linkedin-skills-linkedin-content-references-accessibility-and-inclusion-4c20e9bc))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-marketing-linkedin-skills-linkedin-content-references-hook-and-fold-mechanics-379c8e91**: LinkedIn truncates a post in the feed. On mobile that happens around 140 characters, on desktop around 210, and the reader's decision to press "…see more" is made entirely on what is above that line. Everything you wrote below it is conditional on those two sentences. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-marketing-linkedin-skills-linkedin-content-references-hook-and-fold-mechanics-379c8e91))

- **public-02-claude-skills-main-research-ops-commands-cs-clinical-research-5426bc00**: Clinical study design. Select and classify endpoints, estimate sample size / power (means / proportions / survival), and score a study plan for a GO / GO-WITH-CONDITIONS / REDESIGN / NO-GO phase-gate decision. Every output is an ESTIMATE plus a named clinical owner — never clinical fact. Direct invocation of the clinical-research skill. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-research-ops-commands-cs-clinical-research-5426bc00))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-research-ops-commands-cs-grill-research-ops-5fcf18ce**: Matt Pocock-style docs-anchored grilling for a Research Operations plan — clinical study, R&D budget, market size, or product study. Walks the plan against the research canon (ICH E9, IAS 38, Cochran, Kotler, Nielsen) one question at a time, recommends an answer per question, and refuses to invoke any sub-skill until the lane-defining decisions are locked. Use before running /cs:research-ops on a fuzzy plan. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-research-ops-commands-cs-grill-research-ops-5fcf18ce))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-research-ops-commands-cs-market-research-d20e9826**: Market research methodology. Size a market as TAM/SAM/SOM computed BOTH top-down and bottoms-up (never a single number), plan a survey sample size with finite-population correction and per-segment minimums, and score candidate segments against Kotler's criteria. Outputs always show method + assumptions. Direct invocation of the market-research skill. - **public-02-claude-skills-main-research-ops-commands-cs-product-research-f31fef4a**: Product / user research methodology.
